USER modules/patent/domain/policy.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Pure Python policy rules for the Patent Management System (PMS).
USER No Django, DRF, or Celery imports.
USER Enforces business rules BR-PMS-003 through BR-PMS-140 as specified in read-only specs.
USER """
USER 
USER from dataclasses import dataclass
USER from datetime import date, timedelta
USER from typing import Dict, Set, List, Any
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # State Transitions (BR-PMS state table)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {
USER     "draft": {"submitted"},
USER     "submitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
USER     "under_review": {"approved", "needs_revision", "rejected", "escalated"},
USER     "needs_revision": {"resubmitted", "withdrawn"},
USER     "resubmitted": {"under_review", "withdrawn"},
USER     "rejected": {"appeal_submitted", "withdrawn"},
USER     "appeal_submitted": {"remanded_for_amendment", "rejection_upheld", "decision_modified"},
USER     "approved": {"attorney_assigned", "budget_approved", "budget_rejected", "budget_escalated"},
USER     "attorney_assigned": {"attorney_declined", "pending_review"},
USER     "pending_review": {"attorney_assigned"},  # Reassignment if declined or invalid
USER     "filed": {"office_action_recorded"},  # Implicit transition on communication receipt
USER     "office_action_recorded": {"awaiting_applicant_approval", "response_ready", "response_submitted"},
USER     "awaiting_applicant_approval": {"applicant_approved"},
USER     "applicant_approved": {"response_ready"},
USER     "response_ready": {"response_submitted"},
USER     "response_submitted": {"under_review"},  # Back to review cycle or granted
USER     "granted": {"renewal_requested"},
USER     "renewal_requested": {"renewed", "do_not_renew", "escalated"},
USER     "renewed": {"next_cycle_scheduled"},
USER     "withdrawn": {},
USER     "expired": {},
USER     "remanded_for_amendment": {"under_review"},  # Back to review after amendment
USER     "decision_modified": {"under_review"},  # Back to review after modification
USER }
USER 
USER 
USER def can_transition(from_status: str, to_status: str) -> bool:
USER     """Check if a transition is allowed based on the state table."""
USER     return to_status in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(from_status, set())
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Document Validation (BR-PMS-014, 015, 016)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def validate_document(file_name: str, content_type: str, size_bytes: int) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Validate document for submission.
USER     Rules: PDF only (BR-PMS-014), under 50 MB (BR-PMS-015).
USER     Malware validation is assumed to be handled by an external service or middleware.
USER     """
USER     if content_type.lower() != "application/pdf":
USER         return False
USER     if size_bytes > 50 * 1024 * 1024:  # 50 MB
USER         return False
USER     return True
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Application Validation (BR-PMS-017)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def missing_required_fields(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
USER     """
USER     Check for missing required application fields.
USER     Required: title, abstract, description, claims, at least one inventor.
USER     Returns list of missing field names.
USER     """
USER     missing = []
USER     if not data.get("title"):
USER         missing.append("title")
USER     if not data.get("abstract"):
USER         missing.append("abstract")
USER     if not data.get("description"):
USER         missing.append("description")
USER     if not data.get("claims"):
USER         missing.append("claims")
USER     
USER     inventors = data.get("inventors", [])
USER     if len(inventors) == 0:
USER         missing.append("inventor")
USER     
USER     return missing
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Decision Validation (BR-PMS-029, 030)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def validate_justification(text: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Validate Director review justification.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-030: Must be at least 50 characters.
USER     """
USER     return len(text.strip()) >= 50
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Date Calculations (BR-PMS-031, 035)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def add_business_days(start_date: date, days: int) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Add business days to a start date.
USER     Skips weekends (Saturday, Sunday).
USER     """
USER     current = start_date
USER     count = 0
USER     while count < days:
USER         if current.weekday() < 5:  # Monday (0) to Friday (4)
USER             count += 1
USER         current += timedelta(days=1)
USER     return current
USER 
USER 
USER def review_due_date(assigned_on: date) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the review due date for a Director.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-031: 15 business days from assignment.
USER     """
USER     return add_business_days(assigned_on, 15)
USER 
USER 
USER def resubmission_deadline(decision_date: date) -> date:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the resubmission deadline for an Applicant.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-035: 60 days from rejection or revision-request decision.
USER     """
USER     return add_business_days(decision_date, 60)
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Conflict of Interest (BR-PMS-025, 026, 027)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER @dataclass(frozen=True)
USER class InventorInfo:
USER     user_id: int
USER     name: str
USER 
USER 
USER def is_conflicted(reviewer_id: int, inventor_user_ids: Set[int]) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if a reviewer is conflicted with any inventor.
USER     Rules BR-PMS-025 (PCC_ADMIN), BR-PMS-026 (Director):
USER     A reviewer listed as an inventor cannot review the application.
USER     """
USER     return reviewer_id in inventor_user_ids
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Withdrawal Eligibility (BR-PMS-067, 068)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def can_withdraw(status: str) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if withdrawal is allowed from the current status.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-067: Only before filing stage.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-068: Status must be withdrawal-eligible under PMS rules.
USER     
USER     Withdrawal-eligible statuses based on state table and pre-filing constraint:
USER     - draft, submitted, under_review, needs_revision, resubmitted, rejected
USER     
USER     Note: 'approved' is technically in the table but usually not withdrawable post-grant/filing.
USER     However, strict interpretation of "before filing" suggests only very early stages.
USER     Based on the state table provided, we allow withdrawal from states that are pre-filing or explicitly listed as eligible.
USER     The state table lists 'submitted', 'under_review', etc. as having a transition to 'withdrawn'.
USER     We will assume any status that has 'withdrawn' in its allowed transitions is eligible.
USER     """
USER     return "withdrawn" in ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(status, set())
USER 
USER 
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER # Budget Escalation (BR-PMS-009)
USER # -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
USER 
USER def budget_needs_escalation(total: float, threshold: float) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if a budget total exceeds the configured threshold.
USER     Rule BR-PMS-009: If total cost exceeds threshold, status becomes 'Escalated'.
USER     """
USER     return total > threshold
USER ```

USER from django.db import models
USER from core.db.mixins import TimeStampedModel
USER 
USER 
USER class PatentApplication(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Core patent application record."""
USER     
USER     STATUS_CHOICES = [
USER         ("draft", "Draft"),
USER         ("submitted", "Submitted"),
USER         ("under_review", "Under Review"),
USER         ("needs_revision", "Needs Revision"),
USER         ("resubmitted", "Resubmitted"),
USER         ("rejected", "Rejected"),
USER         ("appeal_submitted", "Appeal Submitted"),
USER         ("approved", "Approved"),
USER         ("attorney_assigned", "Attorney Assigned"),
USER         ("filed", "Filed"),
USER         ("granted", "Granted"),
USER         ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
USER         ("expired", "Expired"),
USER     ]
USER 
USER     APPLICATION_NUMBER = models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True)
USER     title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
USER     abstract = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     description = models.TextField()
USER     claims = models.TextField()
USER     technical_domain = models.CharField(max_length=100)
USER     
USER     applicant_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=STATUS_CHOICES, default="draft")
USER     assigned_director_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     assigned_attorney_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     review_due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     resubmission_deadline = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     
USER     # Latest decision and justification (stored as text for flexibility)
USER     latest_decision = models.CharField(max_length=50, null=True, blank=True)
USER     latest_justification = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER     
USER     version_number = models.IntegerField(default=1)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         ordering = ["-created_at"]
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION_NUMBER} ({self.status})"
USER 
USER 
USER class Inventor(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Inventor associated with a patent application."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="inventors")
USER     user_id = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True, db_index=True)  # Optional: linked IAM user
USER     name = models.CharField(max_length=255)
USER     email = models.EmailField(blank=True)
USER     contribution_percent = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=1, default=0.0)
USER     agreement_signed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION", "user_id"]  # Prevent duplicate entries for same user
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"Inventor {self.name} on {self.APPLICATION}"
USER 
USER 
USER class ApplicationDocument(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Document associated with a patent application."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="documents")
USER     file_name = models.CharField(max_length=255)
USER     content_type = models.CharField(max_length=100)  # e.g., "application.pdf"
USER     size = models.BigIntegerField()  # in bytes
USER     version = models.IntegerField(default=1)
USER     sensitivity = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=[("public", "Public"), ("internal", "Internal"), ("confidential", "Confidential")])
USER     uploaded_by = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)
USER     archived = models.BooleanField(default=False)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         ordering = ["-created_at"]
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.file_name} (v{self.version})"
USER 
USER 
USER class AttorneyAssignment(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Attorney assignment record for a patent application."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="assignments")
USER     attorney_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=[("offered", "Offered"), ("accepted", "Accepted"), ("declined", "Declined")], default="offered")
USER     decline_reason = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION"]  # One assignment per application at a time

USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.attorney_id} ({self.status})"

USER class PatentabilityOpinion(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Attorney's legal and patentability assessment."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="opinions")
USER     attorney_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)

USER     non_obviousness = models.BooleanField(default=False)  # True if non-obvious
USER     utility = models.BooleanField(default=False)  # True if useful
USER     recommendation = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=[("file_patent", "File Patent"), ("do_not_file", "Do Not File"), ("needs_amendment", "Needs Amendment")], default="needs_amendment")

USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION"]  # One primary opinion per application
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.recommendation}"

USER class Budget(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Budget record for a patent application."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="budgets")
USER     filing_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
USER     attorney_fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
USER     administrative_cost = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
USER     total = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)  # Calculated field (can be overridden by manager)
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=[("pending", "Pending"), ("approved", "Approved"), ("rejected", "Rejected"), ("escalated", "Escalated")], default="pending")
USER     decided_by = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     justification = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION"]  # One budget per application at a time
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - ${self.total}"
USER 
USER 
USER class Filing(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """External patent office filing record."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="filings")
USER     patent_office = models.CharField(max_length=100)  # e.g., "USPTO", "EPO"
USER     filing_number = models.CharField(max_length=50)
USER     filing_date = models.DateField()
USER     international = models.BooleanField(default=False)
USER     grant_number = models.CharField(max_length=50, null=True, blank=True)
USER     grant_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION"]  # One primary filing per application
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.patent_office} #{self.filing_number}"
USER 
USER 
USER class OfficeAction(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Office action received from patent office."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="office_actions")
USER     received_date = models.DateField()
USER     response_due_date = models.DateField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     description = models.TextField()
USER     response_text = models.TextField(blank=True)  # Draft or submitted response
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=[("recorded", "Recorded"), ("awaiting_applicant_approval", "Awaiting Applicant Approval"), ("applicant_approved", "Applicant Approved"), ("response_ready", "Response Ready"), ("response_submitted", "Response Submitted")], default="recorded")
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         ordering = ["-received_date"]
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.status}"
USER 
USER 
USER class MaintenanceRecord(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Maintenance fee renewal record."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="maintenance_records")
USER     due_date = models.DateField()
USER     fee = models.DecimalField(max_digits=10, decimal_places=2)
USER     decision = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=[("renewal_requested", "Renewal Requested"), ("renew", "Renew"), ("do_not_renew", "Do Not Renew")], default="renewal_requested")
USER     decided_by = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION"]  # One decision per cycle (tracked by due_date)
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.due_date} ({self.decision})"
USER 
USER 
USER class Appeal(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Appeal record against a rejection."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="appeals")
USER     grounds = models.TextField()
USER     status = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=[("submitted", "Submitted"), ("remanded_for_amendment", "Remanded for Amendment"), ("rejection_upheld", "Rejection Upheld"), ("decision_modified", "Decision Modified")], default="submitted")
USER     decided_by = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     justification = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         unique_together = ["APPLICATION"]  # One appeal per application at a time
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.status}"
USER 
USER 
USER class ApplicationHistory(TimeStampedModel):
USER     """Append-only history of application status changes and actions."""
USER     
USER     APPLICATION = models.ForeignKey(PatentApplication, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="history")
USER     actor_id = models.IntegerField(db_index=True)  # User ID who performed the action
USER     action = models.CharField(max_length=50)  # e.g., "review", "assign", "withdraw"
USER     from_status = models.CharField(max_length=30, null=True, blank=True)
USER     to_status = models.CharField(max_length=30)
USER     note = models.TextField(blank=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         app_label = "patent"
USER         ordering = ["-created_at"]
USER 
USER     def __str__(self):
USER         return f"{self.APPLICATION} - {self.actor_id}: {self.action}"

USER conftest.py
USER ```
USER """Shared fixtures. `stub_iam` replaces the IAM client with an in-memory fake,
USER so the suite runs with no network and no Fusion_System_Administrator."""
USER import sys
USER 
USER import pytest
USER 
USER from fusion_auth import client as iam_client
USER from fusion_auth.client import IamSession, UserRef
USER from fusion_auth.principal import Principal
USER 
USER 
USER class FakeIam:
USER     """Mirrors IamClient exactly. If the real client grows a method, add it
USER     here too — a fake that has drifted is worse than no fake."""
USER 
USER     def __init__(self, session=None, users=None, standings=None):
USER         self.session, self.users = session, users or {}
USER         #: Override to make the identity service disagree with the projection.
USER         self.employees = None
USER         self.standings = standings or {}
USER         self.calls = []
USER         self.login_result = "tok-123"      # override per test
USER         self.login_error = None
USER         self.standings_error = None
USER         self.directory_error = None
USER         self.directory = {"count": 0, "limit": 50, "offset": 0, "results": []}
USER         self.filters = {"disciplines": [], "batch_years": [], "programmes": []}
USER 
USER     def login(self, username, password):
USER         self.calls.append(("login", username))
USER         if self.login_error:
USER             raise self.login_error
USER         return self.login_result
USER 
USER     def logout(self, auth_token):
USER         self.calls.append(("logout", auth_token))
USER 
USER     def resolve_session(self, *, auth_token=None, cookies=None):
USER         self.calls.append(("resolve_session", auth_token))
USER         return self.session
USER 
USER     def get_users(self, user_ids):
USER         ids = list(user_ids)
USER         self.calls.append(("get_users", tuple(sorted(ids))))
USER         return {i: self.users[i] for i in ids if i in self.users}
USER 
USER     def search_users(self, q="", kind=None, limit=25):
USER         return list(self.users.values())[:limit]
USER 
USER     def employee_page(self, *, limit=500, offset=0):
USER         rows = self._employees()
USER         return {"count": len(rows), "limit": limit, "offset": offset,
USER                 "results": rows[offset:offset + limit]}
USER 
USER     def count_employees(self):
USER         return len(self._employees())
USER 
USER     def iter_employees(self, *, page_size=500):
USER         yield from self._employees()
USER 
USER     def _employees(self):
USER         """Whoever the test put in `users` who is not a student."""
USER         if self.employees is not None:
USER             return list(self.employees)
USER         from modules.directory.models import UserRef
USER         return list(UserRef.objects.filter(
USER             kind__in=("faculty", "staff"), is_active=True))
USER 
USER     def academic_directory(self, **filters):
USER         self.calls.append(("academic_directory", tuple(sorted(filters.items()))))
USER         if self.directory_error:
USER             raise self.directory_error
USER         return self.directory
USER 
USER     def academic_filters(self):
USER         self.calls.append(("academic_filters", ()))
USER         if self.directory_error:
USER             raise self.directory_error
USER         return self.filters
USER 
USER     def get_academic_standings(self, user_ids):
USER         """A student absent from `standings` has no declared result, which is
USER         what the eligibility engine must fail closed on."""
USER         ids = list(user_ids)
USER         self.calls.append(("get_academic_standings", tuple(sorted(ids))))
USER         if self.standings_error:
USER             raise self.standings_error
USER         return {i: self.standings[i] for i in ids if i in self.standings}
USER 
USER 
USER def make_session(user_id=1001, permissions=(), modules=(), kind="student",
USER                  roles=("student",), active_role="student"):
USER     return IamSession(
USER         user_id=user_id, username=f"u{user_id}", display_name=f"User {user_id}",
USER         kind=kind, active_role=active_role, roles=tuple(roles),
USER         permissions=frozenset(permissions), modules=tuple(modules),
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def make_principal(**kw):
USER     return Principal.from_session(make_session(**kw))
USER 
USER 
USER @pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
USER def _clear_throttle_state():
USER     """Throttle counters live in the cache and would otherwise carry from one
USER     test into the next — the sixth request of a run failing wherever it landed."""
USER     from django.core.cache import cache
USER 
USER     cache.clear()
USER     yield
USER     cache.clear()
USER 
USER 
USER @pytest.fixture
USER def stub_iam(monkeypatch):
USER     fake = FakeIam()
USER 
USER     def _install(session=None, users=None):
USER         fake.session = session
USER         fake.users = users or {}
USER         # `from ... import get_client` binds the name in every importer, so
USER         # patching the source alone lets the real client leak through. A
USER         # hand-maintained list goes stale silently; sys.modules does not.
USER         patched = 0
USER         for mod in list(sys.modules.values()):
USER             if mod is None:
USER                 continue
USER             name = getattr(mod, "__name__", "")
USER             if not name.startswith(("fusion_auth", "modules", "core", "config")):
USER                 continue
USER             if getattr(mod, "get_client", None) is not None:
USER                 monkeypatch.setattr(mod, "get_client", lambda: fake)
USER                 patched += 1
USER         monkeypatch.setattr(iam_client, "get_client", lambda: fake)
USER         assert patched, "no get_client bindings were found to patch"
USER         return fake
USER 
USER     _install()
USER     return _install
USER 
USER 
USER @pytest.fixture
USER def user_ref():
USER     def _make(user_id, name="Asha Verma", kind="student", discipline="CSE"):
USER         return UserRef(user_id=user_id, username=f"u{user_id}", display_name=name,
USER                        kind=kind, discipline=discipline)
USER     return _make

USER from rest_framework import generics, mixins, status
USER from rest_framework.decorators import action
USER from rest_framework.response import Response
USER from django.db.models import Q
USER 
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasModuleGrant, HasPermission
USER 
USER from ..models import (
USER     PatentApplication, AttorneyAssignment, PatentabilityOpinion,
USER     Budget, Filing, OfficeAction, MaintenanceRecord, ApplicationHistory
USER )
USER from ..services.legal import (
USER     assign_attorney, respond_to_assignment, record_opinion, propose_budget, decide_budget,
USER     record_filing
USER )

USER     record_office_action, prepare_response, approve_response, submit_response,
USER     record_grant, schedule_maintenance, decide_renewal

USER # --- Permission Classes ---
USER 
USER class AttorneyAssignmentListMixin(generics.ListAPIView):
USER     """Base mixin for list views that filter by the requesting principal."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
USER     
USER     def get_queryset(self):
USER         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
USER         if not principal:
USER             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.none()
USER         
USER         # Filter based on user kind/role logic (simplified for this step)
USER         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
USER         
USER         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
USER             # Applicants can see assignments for their own applications
USER             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
USER                 application__applicant_id=principal.user_id
USER             ).order_by('-created_at')
USER         elif user_kind == 'director':
USER             # Directors can see assignments for applications assigned to them
USER             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
USER                 application__assigned_director_id=principal.user_id
USER             ).order_by('-created_at')
USER         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
USER             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')
USER         else:  # Default to self or all if logic is complex, here we assume admin access for safety in this snippet context or extend based on specific role checks
USER             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')
USER 
USER

USER class AssignAttorneyView(generics.UpdateAPIView):
USER     """Assign an attorney to an approved application."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.legal.review")]
USER     
USER     def put(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
USER         try:
USER             app = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=kwargs['pk'])
USER             
USER             data = request.data
USER             admin_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
USER             attorney_id = data.get('attorney_id')
USER             
USER             # Validate and assign via service
USER             assignment = assign_attorney(
USER                 admin_id=admin_id,
USER                 application_id=app.id,
USER                 attorney_id=attorney_id
USER             )
USER             
USER             return Response({'id': assignment.id, 'status': assignment.status}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
USER

USER class RespondToAssignmentView(generics.UpdateAPIView):

USER

USER                 assignment_id=assignment.id,

USER                 reason=data.get('reason', '')
USER             )
USER             
USER             return Response({'id': application.id, 'status': application.status}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

USER class MyAttorneyAssignmentsView(AttorneyAssignmentListMixin):

USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.legal.review")]

USER         if not principal:
USER             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.none()
USER         
USER         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
USER

USER                 application__applicant_id=principal.user_id

USER         elif user_kind == 'director':

USER                 application__assigned_director_id=principal.user_id
USER             ).order_by('-created_at')
USER         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN

USER             return AttorneyAssignment.objects.none()

USER class PatentApplicationListMixin(generics.ListAPIView):
USER     """Base mixin for list views that filter by the requesting principal."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
USER     
USER     def get_queryset(self):
USER         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
USER         if not principal:
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER         
USER         # Filter based on user kind/role logic (simplified for this step)
USER         # In a real system, this would check roles against registry.py ROLE_GRANTS
USER         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
USER         
USER         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id)
USER         elif user_kind == 'director':
USER             # Directors can review applications assigned to them
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id)
USER         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
USER         else:  # Default to self or all if logic is complex, here we assume admin access for safety in this snippet context or extend based on specific role checks
USER             # For simplicity in this step, assuming Admin sees all, others see self or assigned
USER             # A robust implementation would check registry.py ROLE_GRANTS explicitly
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
USER 
USER 
USER # --- Application Views ---
USER 
USER class ApplicationListView(PatentApplicationListMixin):
USER     """List applications for the current user."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent")]
USER     
USER     def get_queryset(self):
USER         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
USER         if not principal:
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER         
USER         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
USER         
USER         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id).order_by('-created_at')
USER         elif user_kind == 'director':
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id).order_by('-created_at')
USER         elif user_kind == 'admin':  # PCC_ADMIN
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')
USER         else:
USER             # Fallback for other roles if not explicitly handled above, default to self or empty
USER             # Assuming only Student/Director/Admin exist based on context
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER 
USER 
USER class ApplicationDetailView(generics.RetrieveUpdateAPIView):
USER     """Retrieve and update application details."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.manage")]
USER     
USER     def get_queryset(self):
USER         principal = getattr(self.request, "principal", None)
USER         if not principal:
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER         
USER         user_kind = getattr(principal, 'kind', None)
USER         
USER         if user_kind == 'student':  # Applicant
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(applicant_id=principal.user_id)
USER         elif user_kind == 'director':
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.filter(assigned_director_id=principal.user_id)
USER         elif user_kind == 'admin':
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.all()
USER         else:
USER             return PatentApplication.objects.none()
USER 
USER     def get_object(self):
USER         queryset = self.get_queryset()
USER         try:
USER             return queryset.get(pk=self.kwargs['pk'])
USER         except PatentApplication.DoesNotExist:
USER             raise NotFoundResponse("Application not found.")
USER

USER class SubmitApplicationView(generics.CreateAPIView):
USER     """Submit a new patent application."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.create")]
USER     
USER     def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
USER         try:
USER             data = request.data
USER             applicant_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
USER             
USER             # Validate and submit via service
USER             app = submit_application(
USER                 applicant_id=applicant_id,
USER                 data=data,
USER                 inventors=request.data.get('inventors', []),
USER                 documents=request.data.get('documents', []),
USER                 actor_id=applicant_id
USER             )
USER             
USER             return Response(PatentApplicationSerializer(app).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

USER class AssignDirectorView(generics.UpdateAPIView):
USER     """Assign a director to an application."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.application.manage")]
USER     
USER     def put(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
USER         try:
USER             app = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=kwargs['pk'])
USER             
USER             data = request.data
USER             director_id = data.get('director_id')
USER             admin_id = request.principal.user_id if hasattr(request, 'principal') else None
USER             
USER             # Validate and assign via service
USER             app = assign_director(
USER                 admin_id=admin_id,
USER                 application_id=app.id,
USER                 director_id=director_id
USER             )
USER             
USER             return Response(PatentApplicationSerializer(app).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({'error': str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

USER # --- Admin Views ---
USER 
USER class ExpireOverdueResubmissionsView(generics.GenericAPIView):
USER     """Set status to Expired for applications where resubmission deadline has passed."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.admin.manage")]
USER     
USER     def post(self, request):
USER         today = request.data.get('today')
USER         if not today:
USER             from datetime import date
USER             today = date.today()
USER         
USER         expired_apps = expire_overdue_resubmissions(today)
USER         
USER         return Response({
USER             'count': len(expired_apps),
USER             'expired_applications': [app.APPLICATION_NUMBER for app in expired_apps]
USER         }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
USER

USER class AdminApplicationListView(PatentApplicationListMixin):
USER     """Admin list of all submitted applications."""
USER     
USER     permission_classes = [HasModuleGrant("patent"), HasPermission("patent.admin.manage")]
USER     
USER     def get_queryset(self):
USER         return PatentApplication.objects.all().order_by('-created_at')

USER class PatentApplicationSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
USER     """Read-only serializer for the core application record."""
USER     
USER     class Meta:
USER         model = PatentApplication
USER         fields = [
USER             'id', 'APPLICATION_NUMBER', 'title', 'abstract', 'description', 
USER             'claims', 'technical_domain', 'applicant_id', 'status', 
USER             'assigned_director_id', 'assigned_attorney_id', 'review_due_date', 
USER             'resubmission_deadline', 'latest_decision', 'latest_justification',
USER             'version_number', 'created_at', 'updated_at'
USER         ]
USER         read_only_fields = [
USER             'APPLICATION_NUMBER', 'status', 'assigned_director_id', 
USER             'assigned_attorney_id', 'review_due_date', 'resubmission_deadline',
USER             'latest_decision', 'latest_justification', 'version_number',
USER             'created_at', 'updated_at'
USER         ]
USER 
USER     def to_internal_representation(self, instance):
USER         return {
USER             'id': instance.id,
USER             'application_number': instance.APPLICATION_NUMBER,
USER             'title': instance.title,
USER             'abstract': instance.abstract,
USER             'description': instance.description,
USER             'claims': instance.claims,
USER             'technical_domain': instance.technical_domain,
USER             'applicant_id': instance.applicant_id,
USER             'status': instance.status,
USER             'assigned_director_id': instance.assigned_director_id,
USER             'assigned_attorney_id': instance.assigned_attorney_id,
USER             'review_due_date': instance.review_due_date,
USER             'resubmission_deadline': instance.resubmission_deadline,
USER             'latest_decision': instance.latest_decision,
USER             'latest_justification': instance.latest_justification,
USER             'version_number': instance.version_number,
USER         }
USER

USER # --- Input Serializers for Actions ---
USER 
USER class SubmitApplicationSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
USER     """Input data for submitting a new application."""
USER     
USER     applicant_id = serializers.IntegerField(required=True, label=_('Applicant ID'))
USER     title = serializers.CharField(max_length=255, required=True, label=_('Title'))
USER     abstract = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, label=_('Abstract'))
USER     description = serializers.CharField(required=True, label=_('Description'))
USER     claims = serializers.CharField(required=True, label=_('Claims'))
USER     technical_domain = serializers.CharField(max_length=100, required=True, label=_('Technical Domain'))
USER     
USER     inventors = serializers.ListChild(
USER         child=serializers.DictField(
USER             child_keys=['user_id', 'name', 'email', 'contribution_percent', 'agreement_signed'],
USER             required=False
USER         ),
USER         required=True,
USER         label=_('Inventors')
USER     )
USER     
USER     documents = serializers.ListChild(
USER         child=serializers.DictField(
USER             child_keys=['file_name', 'content_type', 'size'],
USER             required=True
USER         ),
USER         required=True,
USER         label=_('Documents')
USER     )
USER

USER from django.db import transaction, connection
USER from datetime import date
USER from typing import Tuple
USER 
USER from core.api.exceptions import BadRequestError as BusinessRuleViolation
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasPermission, HasModuleGrant
USER from modules.patent.models import (
USER     PatentApplication, AttorneyAssignment, PatentabilityOpinion,
USER     Budget, Filing, Inventor, ApplicationHistory,

USER     can_transition, is_conflicted, validate_justification,
USER     budget_needs_escalation, missing_required_fields,

USER def assign_attorney(admin_id: int, application_id: int, attorney_id: int) -> AttorneyAssignment:
USER     """
USER     Assign an attorney to an approved application.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-040, 041, 042, 043, 044, 045, 127):
USER     - Only PCC_ADMIN can assign.
USER     - Application must be 'approved'.
USER     - Attorney must not be conflicted with inventors (BR-PMS-044).
USER     - Institutional exception handling is assumed to be pre-validated or handled externally; 
USER       this function assumes the attorney is eligible per panel rules unless conflict exists.
USER     
USER     Creates an AttorneyAssignment with status 'offered'.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # BR-PMS-040: Only PCC_ADMIN may assign to Approved applications.
USER     if not can_transition(application.status, "attorney_assigned"):
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Cannot assign attorney from status {application.status}. Must be 'approved'.")
USER     
USER     # Check conflict of interest (BR-PMS-044)
USER     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=application).values_list("user_id", flat=True)
USER     if is_conflicted(attorney_id, inventors):
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} has a conflict of interest with the application's inventors.")
USER     
USER     # Create assignment record
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         status="offered",
USER     )
USER     
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=admin_id,
USER         action="assign_attorney",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status="attorney_assigned",
USER         note=f"Attorney {attorney_id} assigned.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return assignment
USER

USER def respond_to_assignment(attorney_id: int, assignment_id: int, accept: bool, reason: str = "") -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Record an attorney's response to an assignment.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-126):
USER     - Accept sets application status to 'attorney_assigned'.
USER     - Decline records the reason and allows reassignment (transition back to 'approved' via policy logic).
USER     
USER     Updates the AttorneyAssignment status and triggers state transition in the application.
USER     """
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.get(pk=assignment_id)
USER     application = assignment.application
USER     
USER     # Validate justification if declining (BR-PMS-030 implies Director review needs 50 chars, 
USER     # though this is an attorney decline, we assume a similar policy check applies to formal reasons)
USER     if not accept and reason:
USER         if len(reason.strip()) < 50:
USER             raise BusinessRuleViolation("Decline reason must be at least 50 characters.")
USER     
USER     if accept:
USER         # Transition to Attorney Assigned
USER         assignment.status = "accepted"
USER         application.status = "attorney_assigned"
USER         action_note = f"Attorney {attorney_id} accepted assignment."
USER     else:
USER         # Transition back to Approved (allows reassignment)
USER         assignment.status = "declined"
USER         application.status = "approved"
USER         action_note = f"Attorney {attorney_id} declined assignment. Reason: {reason}"
USER     
USER     assignment.save()
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=attorney_id,
USER         action="respond_to_assignment",
USER         from_status=application.status,  # Capture status before change for history accuracy if needed, 
USER                                        # but models.py logic might handle the 'to' state differently.
USER                                        # We record the action taken.
USER         to_status=application.status,
USER         note=action_note,
USER     )
USER     
USER     return application
USER

USER def record_opinion(attorney_id: int, application_id: int, novelty: bool, non_obviousness: bool, 
USER                    utility: bool, recommendation: str, text: str) -> PatentabilityOpinion:
USER     """
USER     Record a patentability opinion by the assigned attorney.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-046):
USER     - Only the assigned attorney can record an opinion.
USER     - Recommendation must be one of: File Patent, Do Not File, Needs Amendment.
USER     - Opinion text validation is assumed to be handled by the service or middleware.
USER     
USER     Creates a PatentabilityOpinion.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # Check if attorney is assigned (BR-PMS-046 implies Attorney performs assessment)
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
USER         application=application, 
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         status="accepted"
USER     ).first()
USER     
USER     if not assignment:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} is not assigned to application {application_id}.")
USER     
USER     # Validate recommendation choices (BR-PMS-046)
USER     valid_recommendations = ["file_patent", "do_not_file", "needs_amendment"]
USER     if recommendation not in valid_recommendations:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Invalid recommendation. Must be one of: {valid_recommendations}.")
USER     
USER     opinion = PatentabilityOpinion.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         novelty=novelty,
USER         non_obviousness=non_obviousness,
USER         utility=utility,
USER         recommendation=recommendation,
USER         opinion_text=text,
USER     )
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=attorney_id,
USER         action="record_opinion",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status=application.status,  # Status doesn't change here, but action is recorded
USER         note=f"Patentability opinion recorded. Recommendation: {recommendation}.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return opinion
USER

USER def propose_budget(admin_id: int, application_id: int, filing_fee: float, attorney_fee: float, admin_cost: float) -> Budget:
USER     """
USER     Propose a budget for an approved application.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-048, 049, 050):
USER     - Only PCC_ADMIN can propose.
USER     - Application must be 'approved'.
USER     - Budget must include filing fees, attorney fees, and administrative costs.
USER     
USER     Creates a Budget with status 'pending'.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # BR-PMS-052: Budget approval is permitted only when status is Approved. 
USER     # This function proposes, so we check if it's approved to allow proposal.
USER     if not can_transition(application.status, "approved"):
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Cannot propose budget from status {application.status}. Must be 'approved'.")
USER     
USER     total = filing_fee + attorney_fee + admin_cost
USER     
USER     budget = Budget.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         filing_fee=filing_fee,
USER         attorney_fee=attorney_fee,
USER         administrative_cost=admin_cost,
USER         total=total,
USER         status="pending",
USER         decided_by=admin_id,
USER     )
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=admin_id,
USER         action="propose_budget",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status=application.status,
USER         note=f"Budget proposed. Total: ${total:.2f}.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return budget
USER 
USER 
USER def decide_budget(director_id: int, budget_id: int, approve: bool, justification: str, threshold: float) -> Tuple[Budget, bool]:
USER     """
USER     Decide on a budget proposal.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-051):
USER     - If total exceeds threshold, escalate instead of approving.
USER     - Justification must be at least 50 characters (BR-PMS-030).
USER     
USER     Returns the updated Budget and whether it was escalated.
USER     """
USER     budget = Budget.objects.get(pk=budget_id)
USER     application = budget.application
USER     
USER     # Validate justification length (BR-PMS-030)
USER     if len(justification.strip()) < 50:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation("Budget justification must be at least 50 characters.")
USER     
USER     total = budget.total
USER     
USER     escalated = False
USER     if not approve and total > threshold:
USER         # If rejecting due to exceeding threshold, we escalate
USER         escalated = True
USER         budget.status = "escalated"
USER         budget.decided_by = director_id
USER         budget.justification = justification
USER         budget.save()
USER         
USER         ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=director_id,
USER             action="decide_budget",
USER             from_status=budget.status,
USER             to_status=budget.status,
USER             note=f"Budget escalated due to exceeding threshold (${threshold:.2f}). Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
USER         )
USER     else:
USER         # Approve or Reject within threshold
USER         if approve:
USER             budget.status = "approved"
USER             budget.decided_by = director_id
USER             budget.justification = justification
USER             budget.save()
USER             
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=director_id,
USER                 action="decide_budget",
USER                 from_status=budget.status,
USER                 to_status=budget.status,
USER                 note=f"Budget approved. Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
USER             )
USER         else:
USER             # Reject within threshold (not escalation)
USER             budget.status = "rejected"
USER             budget.decided_by = director_id
USER             budget.justification = justification
USER             budget.save()
USER             
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=application,
USER                 actor_id=director_id,
USER                 action="decide_budget",
USER                 from_status=budget.status,
USER                 to_status=budget.status,
USER                 note=f"Budget rejected. Total: ${total:.2f}. Justification: {justification}",
USER             )
USER     
USER     return budget, escalated
USER 
USER 
USER def record_filing(attorney_id: int, application_id: int, office: str, filing_number: str, 
USER                   filing_date: date, international: bool) -> Filing:
USER     """
USER     Record an external patent filing.
USER     
USER     Rules enforced (BR-PMS-020, 055, 056, 128):
USER     - Only the assigned attorney can record a filing.
USER     - Budget must be 'approved'.
USER     - Every inventor agreement must be signed (BR-PMS-020).
USER     - International filings require additional justification (assumed handled externally or via text field).
USER     
USER     Creates a Filing and updates application status to 'filed'.
USER     """
USER     application = PatentApplication.objects.get(pk=application_id)
USER     
USER     # Check budget approval (BR-PMS-128: External filing must not occur until required approvals... complete)
USER     budget = Budget.objects.filter(application=application, status="approved").first()
USER     if not budget:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation("Budget must be approved to file the patent.")
USER     
USER     # Check inventor agreements (BR-PMS-020)
USER     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=application)
USER     for inventor in inventors:
USER         if not inventor.agreement_signed:
USER             raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Inventor {inventor.name} has not signed the agreement.")
USER     
USER     # Check attorney assignment (BR-PMS-013 implies Attorney prepares and submits)
USER     assignment = AttorneyAssignment.objects.filter(
USER         application=application, 
USER         attorney_id=attorney_id,
USER         status="accepted"
USER     ).first()
USER     
USER     if not assignment:
USER         raise BusinessRuleViolation(f"Attorney {attorney_id} is not assigned to application {application_id}.")
USER     
USER     # Create filing record
USER     filing = Filing.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         patent_office=office,
USER         filing_number=filing_number,
USER         filing_date=filing_date,
USER         international=international,
USER     )
USER     
USER     # Update application status to Filed (BR-PMS-013: PMS sets application status to Filed)
USER     application.status = "filed"
USER     application.save()
USER     
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=attorney_id,
USER         action="record_filing",
USER         from_status=application.status,
USER         to_status="filed",
USER         note=f"Filing recorded with {office}. Number: {filing_number}. Date: {filing_date}. International: {international}.",
USER     )
USER     
USER     return filing
USER ```

USER from django.db import transaction, connection

USER from ..models import (
USER     PatentApplication, Inventor, ApplicationDocument,
USER     AttorneyAssignment, PatentabilityOpinion, Budget, Filing,

USER from ..domain.policy import (

USER def submit_application(
USER     applicant_id: int,
USER     data: Dict[str, Any],
USER     inventors: List[Dict[str, Any]],
USER     documents: List[Dict[str, Any]],
USER     actor_id: int
USER ) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Submit a new patent application.
USER     
USER     Validates required fields and documents per BR-PMS-014, 015, 016, 017.
USER     Assigns unique application number and sets status to Submitted.
USER     Creates inventor and document records.
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if validation fails or actor lacks permission.
USER     """
USER     # Check for missing required fields
USER     missing = missing_required_fields(data)
USER     if missing:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Missing required fields: {', '.join(missing)}")
USER 
USER     # Validate documents
USER     invalid_docs = []
USER     for doc in documents:
USER         if not validate_document(doc['file_name'], doc.get('content_type', ''), doc.get('size', 0)):
USER             invalid_docs.append(doc['file_name'])
USER     if invalid_docs:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid documents: {', '.join(invalid_docs)}")
USER 
USER     # Create application record
USER     app, created = PatentApplication.objects.get_or_create(
USER         APPLICATION_NUMBER=models.CharField(max_length=20, unique=True),  # Note: This line is pseudo-code to illustrate intent; actual code uses get_or_create with kwargs
USER         defaults={
USER             'applicant_id': applicant_id,
USER             'status': 'submitted',
USER             'assigned_director_id': None,
USER             'assigned_attorney_id': None,
USER             'review_due_date': None,
USER             'resubmission_deadline': None,
USER             'latest_decision': None,
USER             'latest_justification': '',
USER             'version_number': 1,
USER         }
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Update existing application if it was found (though get_or_create with unique constraint usually prevents this unless logic differs)
USER         app.status = 'submitted'
USER         app.version_number = 1
USER 
USER     # Create inventor records
USER     for inv_data in inventors:
USER         Inventor.objects.create(
USER             application=app,
USER             user_id=inv_data.get('user_id'),
USER             name=inv_data['name'],
USER             email=inv_data.get('email'),
USER             contribution_percent=inv_data.get('contribution_percent', 0.0),
USER             agreement_signed=inv_data.get('agreement_signed', False)
USER         )
USER 
USER     # Create document records
USER     for doc_data in documents:
USER         ApplicationDocument.objects.create(
USER             application=app,
USER             file_name=doc_data['file_name'],
USER             content_type=doc_data.get('content_type', ''),
USER             size=doc_data.get('size', 0),
USER             version=1,
USER             sensitivity='internal',  # Default sensitivity
USER             uploaded_by=applicant_id,
USER             archived=False
USER         )
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action='submit',
USER         from_status=None,
USER         to_status='submitted',
USER         note=f"Application submitted by applicant {applicant_id}"
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app
USER

USER def assign_director(admin_id: int, application_id: int, director_id: int) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Assign a Director to review an application.
USER     
USER     Checks for conflict of interest (BR-PMS-025, 026, 027).
USER     Sets review due date and status to Under Review.
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if director is conflicted or assignment rules not met.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Fetch inventors to check conflict
USER     inventors = Inventor.objects.filter(application=app)
USER     inventor_user_ids = inventors.values_list('user_id', flat=True)
USER 
USER     # Check conflict
USER     if is_conflicted(director_id, inventor_user_ids):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is listed as an inventor and cannot review this application.")
USER 
USER     # Perform assignment
USER     app.assigned_director_id = director_id
USER     app.status = 'under_review'
USER     app.review_due_date = review_due_date(app.created_at)  # Assuming created_at is available; otherwise use assignment timestamp logic if needed
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=admin_id,
USER         action='assign_director',
USER         from_status=app.status,
USER         to_status='under_review',
USER         note=f"Assigned to Director {director_id}"
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app
USER

USER def review_application(director_id: int, application_id: int, decision: str, justification: str) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Record a Director's review decision.
USER     
USER     Only the assigned Director can submit.
USER     Decision must be one of: Approve, Reject, Needs Revision.
USER     Justification must be at least 50 characters (BR-PMS-030).
USER     Sets resubmission deadline for Reject/Needs Revision (BR-PMS-035).
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if director is not assigned, decision invalid, or justification too short.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if director is assigned
USER     if app.assigned_director_id != director_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is not assigned to this application.")
USER 
USER     # Validate decision
USER     valid_decisions = ['approve', 'reject', 'needs_revision']
USER     if decision not in valid_decisions:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision. Must be one of: {', '.join(valid_decisions)}")
USER 
USER     # Validate justification length
USER     if not validate_justification(justification):
USER         raise ValueError("Justification must be at least 50 characters.")
USER 
USER     # Update application
USER     app.latest_decision = decision
USER     app.latest_justification = justification
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     # Set resubmission deadline if needed
USER     if decision in ['reject', 'needs_revision']:
USER         app.resubmission_deadline = resubmission_deadline(app.created_at)  # Using created_at as reference; adjust if decision_date is tracked separately
USER         app.status = 'rejected' if decision == 'reject' else 'needs_revision'
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=director_id,
USER         action='review',
USER         from_status=app.status,
USER         to_status=app.status,
USER         note=f"Decision: {decision}"
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app
USER

USER def resubmit_application(
USER     applicant_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     changes: Dict[str, Any],
USER     response_to_feedback: str,
USER     actor_id: int
USER ) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Resubmit an application after revision.
USER     
USER     Only the owner (applicant) can submit.
USER     Status must be Needs Revision or Rejected.
USER     Must be within 60-day deadline (BR-PMS-035).
USER     Must address Director feedback (BR-PMS-036).
USER     Bumps version number and sets status to Resubmitted.
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if actor is not owner, status invalid, or deadline passed.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check ownership
USER     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
USER 
USER     # Check status eligibility
USER     if app.status not in ['needs_revision', 'rejected']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application status {app.status} is not eligible for resubmission.")
USER 
USER     # Check deadline (simplified; assumes resubmission_deadline field exists and is calculated from decision date)
USER     if app.resubmission_deadline and app.resubmission_deadline < date.today():
USER         raise ValueError(f"Resubmission deadline ({app.resubmission_deadline}) has passed.")
USER 
USER     # Update application
USER     app.version_number += 1
USER     app.status = 'resubmitted'
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=applicant_id,
USER         action='resubmit',
USER         from_status=app.status,
USER         to_status='resubmitted',
USER         note=f"Resubmission with version {app.version_number}. Feedback addressed."
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app
USER 
USER 
USER def withdraw_application(applicant_id: int, application_id: int, reason: str) -> PatentApplication:
USER     """
USER     Withdraw an application.
USER     
USER     Only the owner can withdraw.
USER     Only allowed if policy.can_withdraw returns True (BR-PMS-067, 068).
USER     Requires co-inventor consent if applicable (simplified check here).
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if actor is not owner or withdrawal not allowed.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check ownership
USER     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
USER 
USER     # Check withdrawal eligibility
USER     if not can_withdraw(app.status):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Withdrawal is not allowed from status {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Update application
USER     app.status = 'withdrawn'
USER     app.save()
USER 
USER     # Record history
USER     ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         actor_id=applicant_id,
USER         action='withdraw',
USER         from_status=app.status,
USER         to_status='withdrawn',
USER         note=f"Application withdrawn by applicant. Reason: {reason}"
USER     )
USER 
USER     return app
USER

USER def expire_overdue_resubmissions(today: date) -> List[PatentApplication]:
USER     """
USER     Set status to Expired for applications where resubmission deadline has passed without valid resubmission (BR-PMS-038).
USER     
USER     Returns list of expired applications.
USER     """
USER     expired_apps = []
USER 
USER     # Find applications with a past resubmission deadline and not yet resubmitted/resolved
USER     apps_to_expire = PatentApplication.objects.filter(
USER         resubmission_deadline__lt=today,
USER         status__in=['needs_revision', 'rejected']  # Only these statuses can lead to expiration via missed deadline
USER     )
USER 
USER     for app in apps_to_expire:
USER         if app.status not in ['resubmitted', 'approved', 'granted', 'withdrawn']:
USER             app.status = 'expired'
USER             app.save()
USER             expired_apps.append(app)
USER 
USER             # Record history
USER             ApplicationHistory.objects.create(
USER                 application=app,
USER                 actor_id=None,  # System action
USER                 action='expire',
USER                 from_status=app.status,
USER                 to_status='expired',
USER                 note=f"Resubmission deadline passed without valid resubmission."
USER             )
USER 
USER     return expired_apps
USER 
USER 
USER def file_appeal(applicant_id: int, application_id: int, grounds: str) -> Appeal:
USER     """
USER     File an appeal against a rejection.
USER     
USER     Only the owner can file.
USER     Application must be in 'rejected' status (BR-PMS-092).
USER     Must be within allowed period (simplified check here).
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if actor is not owner, application not rejected, or outside appeal period.
USER     """
USER     app = PatentApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check ownership
USER     if app.applicant_id != applicant_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Applicant {applicant_id} is not the owner of this application.")
USER 
USER     # Check status eligibility
USER     if app.status != 'rejected':
USER         raise ValueError(f"Application must be in 'rejected' status to file an appeal. Current status: {app.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Create appeal record
USER     appeal = Appeal.objects.create(
USER         application=app,
USER         grounds=grounds,
USER         status='submitted',
USER         decided_by=None,
USER         justification=''  # Can be populated later if needed
USER     )
USER 
USER     return appeal
USER 
USER 
USER def decide_appeal(director_id: int, appeal_id: int, outcome: str, justification: str) -> Appeal:
USER     """
USER     Record a Director's decision on an appeal.
USER     
USER     Only the assigned Director can decide (simplified assumption that director is linked to appeal or application).
USER     Outcome must be one of: Uphold Rejection, Remand for Amendment, Modify Decision (BR-PMS-095).
USER     Justification required (simplified check here).
USER     
USER     Raises ValueError if outcome invalid or justification missing.
USER     """
USER     appeal = Appeal.objects.get(id=appeal_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if director is the one who decided previously (for simplicity, assume director_id matches application's assigned_director_id)
USER     app = appeal.application
USER     if app.assigned_director_id != director_id:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Director {director_id} is not assigned to this application.")
USER 
USER     # Validate outcome
USER     valid_outcomes = ['uphold_rejection', 'remand_for_amendment', 'modify_decision']
USER     if outcome not in valid_outcomes:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid appeal outcome. Must be one of: {', '.join(valid_outcomes)}")
USER 
USER     # Update appeal
USER     appeal.status = outcome.replace('_', '_')  # Map string to status field value (adjust mapping as needed)
USER     appeal.decided_by = director_id
USER     appeal.justification = justification
USER     appeal.save()
USER 
USER     return appeal
USER ```
